-- discord.gg/syncrypt

local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

						return string.lower(p119.textObj and p119.textObj.Text or p119.label or "")
					end

					local t43 = {}

					for _, v in ipairs(t42) do
						t43[v] = v612 and string.find(v617(v), v611, 1, true) ~= nil
					end

						if v612 then
							if v.frame:GetAttribute("PreSearchVisible") == nil then
								v.frame:SetAttribute("PreSearchVisible", v.frame.Visible)
							end

							if not v623 then
								local v624, v625, v626 = ipairs(t42)
								local v627 = geniter(v624, v625, v626)

								while true do
									local v628 = table.pack(v627())

										break
									end

											v623 = true

						else

						else

	do
		local t69 = {
			Fly = {
				Enabled = false,
				Speed = 1
			},
			CFSpeed = {
				Enabled = false,

			},
			Noclip = false,

		v43(UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
			if t69.InfJump then
				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character then
					local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

					if Humanoid then
						Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		v43(RunService.Stepped, function(p286, p287)
			if t69.Noclip and LocalPlayer.Character then
				for _, child in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide then
						child.CanCollide = false
					end
				end
			end

			if t69.CFSpeed.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
				local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
					HumanoidRootPart.CFrame += Humanoid.MoveDirection * t69.CFSpeed.Speed * math.min(p287, 0.1) * 60
					HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end

			if t69.Bhop and LocalPlayer.Character then
				local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if (not t69.BhopHoldKey or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)) and Humanoid and Humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and Humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
					Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
				end
			end

			if t69.Jump.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
				local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					if not Humanoid.UseJumpPower then
						Humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
					end

					Humanoid.JumpPower = t69.Jump.Power
				end
			end
		end)
		v43(RunService.RenderStepped, function(p288)
			if t69.Fly.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
				local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid then
					local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					local v1756 = t69.Fly.Speed * math.min(p288, 0.1) * 60
					local vector3 = Vector3.zero

					if v52 then
						vector3 = Humanoid.MoveDirection
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
						vector3 += CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
						vector3 -= CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
						vector3 -= CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
						vector3 += CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
						vector3 += Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
						vector3 -= Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					end

					if vector3.Magnitude > 0 then
						HumanoidRootPart.CFrame += vector3 * v1756
					end

					HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
					Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics)
				end
			end
		end)

							end)
						end)
						TextButton3.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
							task.spawn(function()

					local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	do
		local sessionManagement = createFrame2(v75, "Session Management")

		local function v1770()
			local queue_on_teleport_ = syn and syn.queue_on_teleport or queue_on_teleport or fluxus and fluxus.queue_on_teleport

			if queue_on_teleport_ then
				local LoaderUrl = t7.LoaderUrl

				if type(LoaderUrl) == "string" and LoaderUrl:match("^https?://") and not LoaderUrl:find("YOUR_SCRIPT") then
					queue_on_teleport_("loadstring(game:HttpGet(\"" .. LoaderUrl .. "\"))()")
				else
					pcall(function()
						game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
							Title = "Auto-Load Failed",
							Text = "Loader URL is not set, so Noctis won't reload itself after the hop. Execute it again in the new server.",
							Duration = 10
						})
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		createTextLabel2(sessionManagement, "Rejoin Server", function()
			v1770()

			end)

			local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame", ScreenGui)

			Frame2.Name = "NoctisCmdBar"
			Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 500, 0, 40)
			Frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
			Frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -60)
			Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = t9.Background
			Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame2.ZIndex = 9000
			Frame2.Visible = false
			Instance.new("UICorner", Frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

			local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame2)

			UIStroke.Color = t9.Main
			UIStroke.Thickness = 1.5

			local TextLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame2)

			TextLabel3.Text = ">"
			TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0)
			TextLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
			TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel3.TextColor3 = t9.MainText
			TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			TextLabel3.TextSize = 14
			TextLabel3.ZIndex = 9001

			local TextBox = Instance.new("TextBox", Frame2)

			TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, -8)
			TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 4)
			TextBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
			TextBox.TextSize = 13
			TextBox.TextColor3 = t9.Text
			TextBox.PlaceholderText = "tp name  |  lock name  |  speed 30  |  god  |  esp  |  unload"
			TextBox.PlaceholderColor3 = t9.SubText
			TextBox.Text = ""
			TextBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
			TextBox.ZIndex = 9001
			TextBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

			local u1979 = false

			local function v1980()
				u1979 = false
				TweenService:Create(Frame2, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
					Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -60)
				}):Play()
				task.delay(0.15, function()
					Frame2.Visible = false
					TextBox.Text = ""
				end)
				UserInputService:ReleaseFocus()
			end
			local function v1981()
				if not u1375 then
					return
				end

				u1979 = true
				Frame2.Visible = true
				Frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -60)
				TweenService:Create(Frame2, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
					Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 12)
				}):Play()
				task.delay(0.2, function()
					if u1979 then
						TextBox:CaptureFocus()
					end
				end)
			end
			local function v1982(p332)
				local parts = p332:split(" ")
				local v1984 = string.lower(parts[1] or "")
				local v1985 = table.concat(parts, " ", 2)

				if v1984 == "tp" then
					local v1986 = v1380(v1985)

					if v1986 and v1986.Character then
						if v1986.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if HumanoidRootPart then
								HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v1986.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(2, 0, 0)
							end
						end
					end
				elseif v1984 == "lock" then
					local v1988 = v1380(v1985)

					if v1988 then
						u1373 = v1988
						v1379(v1988)
					end
				elseif v1984 == "speed" then
					local v1989 = tonumber(v1985)

					if v1989 then
						local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if Humanoid then
							Humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(v1989, 0, 500)
						end
					end
				elseif v1984 == "god" then
					u1973 = not u1973
				elseif v1984 ~= "esp" then
					if v1984 == "unload" then
						t8.Unload()
					end
				elseif t8.Setters.ESPEnabled then
					t8.Setters.ESPEnabled(not t59.ESP.Enabled)
				else
					t59.ESP.Enabled = not t59.ESP.Enabled
				end
			end

			TextBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
				if enterPressed and TextBox.Text ~= "" then
					pcall(function()
						v1982(TextBox.Text)
					end)
				end

				v1980()
			end)
			v43(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(p333, p334)
				if p334 then
					return
				end

				if p333.KeyCode ~= Semicolon then
					if p333.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and u1979 then
						v1980()
					end
				elseif u1979 then
					v1980()
				else
					v1981()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	if t10[1] then
		t10[1].TextColor3 = t9.MainText
		t11[1].Visible = true
	end

	do
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(1.5)

			local userStatus = u54 and "Premium" or "Free"

			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Noctis Loaded, " .. LocalPlayer.DisplayName,
					Text = "User Status: " .. userStatus,
					Duration = 5
				})
			end)
			task.wait(2)

			if not getgenv().NoctisInfoShown then
				getgenv().NoctisInfoShown = true
				pcall(function()
					local s21 = "To close the GUI press Right Shift or change from settings."

					if v52 then
						s21 = "Press the button on the right to toggle the UI."
					end

					game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
						Title = "Information",
						Text = s21,
						Duration = 100000,
						Button1 = "Don't Show Again"
					})
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end, v4)
